Work out $495 \div 9$

<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
 &= \dfrac {495} {9} \\\\
 &= \dfrac {55 \times 9} {9} \\\\
 &= \dfrac {55 \times \cancel {9}} {\cancel {9}} \\\\
 &= 55
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

$55$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2AF9BF1A-6E92-4492-9B1C-3C71893FDA24'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Eadie’s favourite television programme started $1 \ \text{hour}$ and $15 \ \text{minutes}$ ago. What time was this? Write your answer in either $12$ or $24 \ \text{hour}$ clock.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text { Programme started}   &= 03 {:} 35 \ \text {pm} - 1 \ \text {hour} \ 15 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                             &= 02 {:} 20 \ \text {pm}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$02 {:} 20 \ \text {pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text { Programme started}   &= 03 {:} 35 \ \text {pm} - 1 \ \text {hour} \ 15 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                             &= 02 {:} 20 \ \text {pm}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$02 {:} 20 \ \text {pm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-74EEEB13-FE38-4F2F-92DC-B391E32A54D9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Eadie has to catch a train at $1710$. How long does she have before the train leaves?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Time left}   &= 17 {:} 10 - 15 {:} 35 \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 35 \ \text {minutes}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1 \ \text {hour} \ 35 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Time left}   &= 17 {:} 10 - 15 {:} 35 \\\\
                    &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 35 \ \text {minutes}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

&= 1  \dfrac {3} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {103} {100} \\\\
&= 1.03
\end {aligned}
$

$1.03$

</div>

$

\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-37A3DF42-1B85-4931-843A-6A0DA7076F88'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$25 \times \ldots = 300$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
25 \times a               &= 300 \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {300} {25} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {12 \times 25} {25} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {25}} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
a                         &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
25 \times a               &= 300 \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {300} {25} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {12 \times 25} {25} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {25}} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
a                         &= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$12$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-66F011FD-DFE1-4337-8556-F1E6A13528AA'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$800 \div \ldots = 20$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
800 \div a               &= 20 \\\\
\dfrac {800} {a}         &= 20 \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {800} {20} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {40 \times 20} {20} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {40 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel{20}} \\\\
a                        &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$40$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
800 \div a               &= 20 \\\\
\dfrac {800} {a}         &= 20 \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {800} {20} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {40 \times 20} {20} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {40 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel{20}} \\\\
a                        &= 40
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-EBA5AF22-8DEA-43F8-A6C6-CA6BCB94F1E3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Bill has a bag containing $60$ sweets. He eats a half of the sweets himself and then gives away a third of the sweets that remain. How many sweets does Bill have left?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Sweets left}  &= \text {Total number of sweets} - (\text {Sweets Bill ate} + \text {Sweets he gives away}) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {of} \ 60 + \dfrac {1} {3} \ \text {of} \ \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {of} \ 60 \bigg) \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \times 60 +\dfrac {1} {3} \times \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \times 60 \bigg) \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {60} {2} + \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {60} {2} \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {30 \times 2} {2} + \dfrac {10 \times 3 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {30 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} + \dfrac {10 \times \cancel 3 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 3 \times \cancel 2} \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - (30 + 10) \\\\
                     &= 60 - 40 \\\\
                     &= 20
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Sweets left}  &= \text {Total number of sweets} - (\text {Sweets Bill ate} + \text {Sweets he gives away}) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {of} \ 60 + \dfrac {1} {3} \ \text {of} \ \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {of} \ 60 \bigg) \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \times 60 +\dfrac {1} {3} \times \bigg(\dfrac {1} {2} \times 60 \bigg) \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {60} {2} + \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {60} {2} \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {30 \times 2} {2} + \dfrac {10 \times 3 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - \bigg(\dfrac {30 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} + \dfrac {10 \times \cancel 3 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 3 \times \cancel 2} \bigg) \\\\
                     &= 60 - (30 + 10) \\\\
                     &= 60 - 40 \\\\
                     &= 20
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7CF85E54-C3FE-4D36-8D4E-EFDA111557AD'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

**Questions** $\bold{36}$ **to** $\bold{38}$ **relate to the triangle numbers**.

The Fibonacci numbers are a sequence of numbers formed so that the first two
numbers are $0$ and $1$, and each subsequent number is the sum of the two previous numbers.

So the first eight terms of the sequence are:

$0, 1, 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13$

What is the next Fibonacci number?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR:  Let's find the relation between the consecutive terms of the sequence.


The next term is calculated by adding together the last two terms. Hence, the sequence would be:

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(0 + 0)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(0 + 1)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(1 + 1)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(1 + 2)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(2 + 3)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(3 + 5)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(5 + 8)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(8 + 13)} }  \\
0, && 1,  &&  1, &&  2, &&  3, &&  5,  &&   8,  &&  13,  && \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Missing number}       &= 8 + 13 \\\\
                            &= 21
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$21$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_sequence_find_difference_note

The next term is calculated by adding together the last two terms. Hence, the sequence would be:

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(0 + 0)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(0 + 1)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(1 + 1)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(1 + 2)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(2 + 3)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(3 + 5)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(5 + 8)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(8 + 13)} }  \\
0, && 1,  &&  1, &&  2, &&  3, &&  5,  &&   8,  &&  13,  && \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Missing number}       &= 8 + 13 \\\\
                            &= 21
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6226AC8D-FE2D-4404-80C6-B5657566AEF2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The fourteenth Fibonacci number is $377$ and the fifteenth Fibonacci number is
$610$.

What is the sixteenth Fibonacci number?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Sixteenth Fibonacci number}  &= \text {Fourteenth Fibonacci number} + \text {Fifteenth Fibonacci number} \\\\
                                    &= 377 + 610 \\\\
                                    &= 987
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$987$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Sixteenth Fibonacci number}  &= \text {Fourteenth Fibonacci number} + \text {Fifteenth Fibonacci number} \\\\
                                    &= 377 + 610 \\\\
                                    &= 987
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$987$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


</div>

<div class='question question'>

What is the thirteenth Fibonacci number?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Thirteenth Fibonacci number}  &= \text {Fifteenth Fibonacci number} - \text {Fourteenth Fibonacci number} \\\\
                                     &= 610 - 377 \\\\
                                     &= 233
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$233$

</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5AD43B4B-E920-4612-9106-152878ACCB28'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Bill is twice as old as his son, who is $50$ years younger than Bill’s father. If the total of their ages is $142$, how old is Bill?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let Bill’s father age $= a$

Bill’s son age $= a - 50$

Bill’s age $= 2 \times (a - 50)$

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
a + (a - 50) + 2 \times (a - 50)                                         &= 142 \\\\
a + a - 50 + 2a - 100                                                    &= 142 \\\\
4a - 150                                                                 &= 142 \\\\
4a                                                                       &= 142 + 150 \\\\
4a                                                                       &= 292 \\\\
a                                                                        &= \dfrac {292} {4} \\\\
a                                                                        &= \dfrac {73 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a                                                                        &= \dfrac {73 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                                                                        &= 73 \\\\
\text {Bill’s age}                                                       &= 2 \times (a - 50) \\\\
                                                                         &= 2 \times (73 - 50) \\\\
                                                                         &= 2 \times 23 \\\\
                                                                         &= 46
\end {aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$46$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let Bill’s father age $= a$

Bill’s son age $= a - 50$

Bill’s age $= 2 \times (a - 50)$

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
a + (a - 50) + 2 \times (a - 50)                                         &= 142 \\\\
a + a - 50 + 2a - 100                                                    &= 142 \\\\
4a - 150                                                                 &= 142 \\\\
4a                                                                       &= 142 + 150 \\\\
4a                                                                       &= 292 \\\\
a                                                                        &= \dfrac {292} {4} \\\\
a                                                                        &= \dfrac {73 \times 4} {4} \\\\
a                                                                        &= \dfrac {73 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
a                                                                        &= 73 \\\\
\text {Bill’s age}                                                       &= 2 \times (a - 50) \\\\
                                                                         &= 2 \times (73 - 50) \\\\
                                                                         &= 2 \times 23 \\\\
                                                                         &= 46
\end {aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$46$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
